    -- ═══════ Proto 349 ═══════  depth=2 params=0 consts=17 instrs=31 subs=0
    -- Key strings: ['PlayerGui', 'EnabledPvP', 'PvpDisabled', 'CommF_', 'wait']
    function EnabledPvP()
      waited = wait(tmp)
      for _, data in pairs(waited) do
        for _, data in pairs(_G.EnabledPvP) do
          tmp = waited
          waited = waited.PvpDisabled
